Objective function in unit sum: score(p)=2n*max(c)

Instant score: use FFT convolution. c = real(ifft(F*F)[:2n-1]); c=np.maximum(c,0). Due numeric negative clamp. Score = 2n*max(c)/S². For unit sum S=1. For speed, normalize to sum1 before FFT.

2. Coordinate / block descent:
   For a peak lag k, we can adjust p along direction that reduces c_k while conserving sum: e.g. move mass from index j contributing heavily to k to direction. Since nonconvex, local line search. Could be effective to break plateaus.

We can perform softmax update v_j += λ d_j? Since derivative of c_k is d_j; to reduce c_k, increase v_j with -d_j. That's same as gradient with weights top. So smooth method covers.

4. LP/parallel tangent method (SOTA) implementation as candidate generator.
Given p with M=score/(2n). We can attempt to maximize sum b subject to (p*b)≤M and b≥0, sum? This is LP. We can solve approximately with projected gradient/mirror descent in log b maybe.
Primal variables b (nonnegative not normalized). Constraints r_k=∑ p_i b_{k-i} ≤ M. Max sum. Since p fixed nonnegative. This is like maximizing a linear objective under a set of linear inequalities. We can use multiplicative weights / exponentiated gradients:
   v_b log variables, b=softmax(v_b)*S? But constraints linear nonhomogeneous. Use projected gradient onto {b≥0, p*b≤M}. Projection onto these constraints with box and convolution inequalities is nontrivial, but can use barrier: maximize log? Maybe use dual.
Dual: min M ∑ y_k s.t. ∑_{k} p_{k-i} y_k ≥1 for all i, y≥0. We can solve dual with projected gradient: minimize F(y)=M∑ y, constraints A^T y ≥1. Projection onto y≥0 and A^T y≥1 (dense) still hard.
But we can solve primal via Lagrangian: For λ_k≥0, maximize ∑ b_i -∑ λ_k (A b)_k + μ_i b_i? Since objective linear, unbounded unless constraints dual. Not.

Helpers:
- next_pow2(x)
- safe_score(a): normalize? It should accept unit-sum maybe. To be consistent, unit_sum_score(a): a=np.asarray float; a=np.maximum(a,0); S=sum; if S<=1e-300 inf; if not finite inf; p=a/S; n=len; m=next_pow2(2n-1); F=fft(p,m); b=ifft(F*F).real[:2n-1]; b=np.maximum(0,b); maxb=b.max(); return 2*n*maxb.
  Note if sequence not unit sum, score scale invariant using normalized.
- score_fft_nonunit for speed: same.
- interp_weights(prev,n): linear interp of weights, ensure nonnegative sum.
- interp_v(prev,n): maybe interpolate log weights.
- normalize(v) stable: v = v - mean(v); clip [-50,50]; p= v; shift max; exp; p/=sum; return p, v? We can keep v centered/clipped.
- softmax(v): stable.
- basis_matrix(n, K): polynomial centered and maybe Fourier. Precompute features.
- param_score(v_params, B, n): p=softmax(B@params + v0?) But B can include bias? Omit bias for invariance. Actually with softmax, adding constant no effect. Use B shape n×K. p = exp(B@c)/sum. Score.
- grad_params(c, B, grad_v): B.T @ grad_v.
- optimize_params(c0, B, n, iters, ...): Adam with exact score, line search? Simpler fixed step, restart.
- full optimizers:
   * full_score_and_c(p,n,m) returns score,c,M.
   * gradient_weights_from_c(c, M, tau, topk): returns w length L.

      Wait if p unit sum and w sum=1, g_i is derivative wrt p_i of weighted sum. For c_k positive. If weights sum=1, S=∑w c. ### Score and convolution

```
def _next_pow2(x):
    n=int(max(1,x)); p=1
    while p<n: p*=2
    return p
```

`fft_conv_square(p, m=None)`:
```
def _conv_self(p, m=None):
    n=p.size
    if m is None: m=_next_pow2(2*n-1)
    F=np.fft.fft(p, m)
    b=np.fft.ifft(F*F).real[:2*n-1]
    b[b<0]=0
    return b
```
Potential F*F can be complex, ifft small negative. Clamp.

Score:
```
def _score_unit(p):
   s=p.sum(); if s<=1e-300: return nan
   if s !=1: p=p/s
   score = 2*n*_conv_self(p).max()
   return float(score)
```
For speed, we can have `_score_and_conv(p)` returning score, c, M:
```
def _score_c(p,m=None):
 n=p.size; S=p.sum(); if S<... inf
 p=p/S; c=_conv_self(p,m); M=c.max(); return 2*n*M, c, M
```
But p already unit sum; avoid renormalizing copy. We'll maintain unit p. For safety, if sum not 1, divide.

Softmax:
```
def _softmax(v):
   v=np.asarray(v,float).copy()
   v = v - np.mean(v)
   v = np.where(np.isfinite(v), v, -20)
   v = v - np.max(v)
   p=np.exp(np.clip(v, -745, 700))
   s=p.sum()
   if s<=0 or not finite: p=np.full(n,1/n)
   else p/=s
   return p
```
For full optimizer, we can maintain v centered and clipped. To reduce overhead, use in-place functions? Top-level.

### Gradient correlation

Need q length n given p and w length 2n-1.
```
def _grad_for_weights(p, w, m=None):
    n=p.size; L=2*n-1
    if m is None: m=_next_pow2(3*n-2)
    pr=p[::-1]
    Fp=np.fft.fft(pr, m)
    Fw=np.fft.fft(w, m)
    q=np.fft.ifft(Fp*Fw).real[n-1:2*n-2]
    # q can have tiny negative due numerical; derivative should be nonnegative? q nonnegative because p,w nonnegative. clamp.
    np.maximum(q,0,out=q)
    return 2.0*q
```

### Weights

```
def _weights_from_c(c, M, temp_frac, top_frac, rng):
   L=c.size
   x=(c-M)/ (M*temp_frac + 1e-300)  # <=0
   x = x - x.max() (max 0)
   w=np.exp(np.clip(x, -60,60))
   ws=w.sum()
   if ws<=0 or not finite: top indices = argsort? else w/=ws
   # top bias
   topk=max(1,int(top_frac*L))
   idx=np.argpartition(c, -topk)[-topk:]
   w[idx] += 1.0
   ws=w.sum(); w/=ws
   return w
```
If M small, temp_frac=0.05 gives x between -20 and0; top weights ~e^{-20} for far; top few. We can set temp_fracs as list or schedule: decrease over optimize. `tau = M * (0.25 +0.25*progress?)`? If focusing max, smaller tau. But too small gradient dominated by one lag; may oscillate. Use 0.10 average. Random each iter? previous used random. We can choose temp_frac = 0.08 +0.08*(1-progress) plus occasional larger to escape. Also topk bias 1.0.

Could use only top K with uniform weights for sharper. Maybe combine: if progress>0.5, topk=32, uniform top with rank weights. But soft all okay.

### Full optimizer

```
def optimize_full(a, n, max_seconds, seed, init_v=None):
   a=np.asarray float; a=np.maximum(a,0); s=sum; p=a/s if>0 else uniform
   rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
   v=np.log(p+1e-15); v-=mean; clip[-20,20]
   m_c=next_pow2(2n-1); m_g=next_pow2(3n-2)
   best=p.copy(); best_score=_score_unit(p)
   mu=zeros; nu=zeros; t=0
   last=now
   lr0=0.18 maybe
   tempinit, tempend: 0.22, 0.04
   check every iter.
   while now<deadline:
       p=_softmax(v)  # maybe use cached p from previous? we can keep p
       # compute c
       F=np.fft.fft(p, m_c)
       c=ifft(F*F).real[:L]; c[max0]
       M=c.max(); if not finite break
       score=2*n*M
       if score<best_score-1e-12: update
       progress=(now-start)/budget
       temp=teminit + (temend-teminit)*progress
       if random<0.1: temp*=uniform(1,3)
       w=_weights_from_c(c,M,temp, top_frac=0.05, rng)
       g=_grad_for_weights(p,w,m_g)
       gm = dot(p,g)
       grad_v = p*(g-gm)
       gm_rms=sqrt(mean(grad_v**2))
       if not finite or gm_rms<1e-18: perturb continue
       d=grad_v/gm_rms
       t+=1
       mu=... ; nu=...
       step=mhat/(sqrt(vhat)+1e-8)
       step=np.clip(step,-1,1)
       lr=lr0*(1-0.7*progress)
       # If gradient direction points to increase score? We'll use negative step; but can flip if evaluation says? We'll assume.
       v_trial = v - lr*step
       v_trial -= mean; clip
       # Evaluate new score with helper using p_new? We'll compute in next loop, but to ensure improvement use line search maybe.
       # Simpler: update v=v_trial; continue; if no improve stuck perturb.
       v=v_trial
   return best, best_score
```

### Coarse parameter optimizer

Implement `optimize_params` for low-dim.
Detailed:
```
def _param_basis(n,K=8):
   t=np.linspace(0,1,n,dtype=float)
   cols=[]
   # omit constant
   cols.append(t)
   cols.append(t*t - 1/3?) maybe centered to improve
   cols.append(t**3 - t/?)
   # centered polynomials centered at 0.5 to reduce correlation
   x=t-0.5
   cols.append(x)
   cols.append(x*x)
   cols.append(x*x*x)
   cols.append(x**4)
   # low Fourier
   cols.append(np.cos(2*np.pi*t))
   cols.append(np.sin(2*np.pi*t))
   cols.append(np.cos(4*np.pi*t))
   # maybe endpoint logs
   cols.append(np.log((t+0.01)((1-t)+0.01)))
   return stack
```

```
def interp_weights(a,n):
   a=np.maximum(a,0); S=sum; if S<=0 uniform
   x_old=np.linspace(0,1,a.size); x_new=np.linspace(0,1,n)
   b=np.interp(x_new,x_old,a/S)
   b=np.maximum(b,0); ss=b.sum(); if ss>0 b/=ss else uniform
   return b
```

Now, maybe LP tangent at small n to generate starts. Let's implement `_lp_stage_small(p, budget, seed)` where p length maybe 256 or 512. Use scipy if available.
Detailed:
```
def _lp_tangent_steps(p, max_iters=6, blend=0.25, allow_short=True):
   p normalized
   best=p; best_score=_score_unit(p)
   for it in range(max_iters):
      c=_conv_self(p); M=c.max()
      # choose constraints all? Build A dense.
      A = _build_convolution_matrix(p)  # shape L,n, p[k-i]
      try:
         res=linprog(-np.ones(n), A_ub=A, b_ub=np.full(L,M), bounds=[(0,None)]*n, method='highs', options={'presolve':True})
      except: break
      if res.success:
         b=res.x; b=np.maximum(b,0); sb=b.sum()
         if sb>1e-12:
            g=b/sb
            # blend
            t=blend*(0.5+0.5*random?)
            q=(1-t)*best + t*g
            q=np.maximum(q,0); sq=q.sum(); if sq>0 q/=sq
            sc=_score_unit(q)
            if sc<best_score:
                best=q; best_score=sc; p=q
   return best, best_score
```

```
def _score_from_v(v, m_c):
   p=_softmax(v)
   F=np.fft.fft(p, m_c)
   b=ifft(F*F).real[:2p.size-1]
   return 2*p.size*max(b)
```
This expects v not centered; softmax handles. Since v length n.
We can cache p and c after accepted move? In loop, after backtracking, we can set v and then at top recompute p/c. To avoid double, we can compute p_new from last try, but simpler recompute.

Let's implement limited backtracking every 5 iterations to save. But robust with simple: Use adaptive lr based on improvement. best_trial_score = current_score; best_trial_v = v

### Coarse parameter optimizer details
Since it uses same full-space gradient methods but on B@theta, we can reuse helper functions. We need compute p from v = B@theta; gradient params = B.T @ grad_v. For backtracking, score of v. We can implement a loop similar to full optimizer but theta update direct.
Pseudo:
```
def optimize_theta(theta, B, n, max_seconds, seed):
   rng...
   th=theta.copy(); best_th=th; best_score=_score_from_v(B@th)
   mu=zeros(K); nu=zeros(K); lr=0.5? Since params not huge. t=0
   while deadline:
      v=B@th; p=softmax(v); c=... M; score=current update.
      temp... weights; g; grad_v=p*(g-dot(p,g))
      grad_theta=B.T@grad_v
      grms=sqrt(mean(grad_theta**2)); if <eps perturb
      d=grad_theta/grms
      Adam; step=...
      move=-lr*step
      backtrack multipliers [1,0.5,0.25,0.125]
      score_try for th+mult*move (v=B@th_try)
      accept best; update.
   return best_th, best_score, B
```
lr in theta: parameter scale maybe ±5; gradient theta normalized, step RMS1, lr 0.2 maybe. A theta step 0.2 changes v by B@0.2; if B cols O(0.1-0.5), v changes 0.05-0.1. Good. lr start 0.5? Could be too large. Use 0.3 initial.
Parameter vectors random uniform [-3,3]. If all zeros uniform. K=8.
Time: n_small=128. B small. Score eval fast. We can run 30 restarts * 2s =60s. Good.
We also include a deterministic initial: geometric/increasing from small; maybe no need.

### Parameter basis functions choice
Need basis covering asym with curvature. Use centered polynomial powers up to degree 5 and low Fourier. But high degree polynomials can be ill-conditioned; use orthonormal-ish with scale. Let x = t - 0.5. Columns:
1: x
2: x^2
3: x^3
4: x^4
5: x^5
6: cos(2πt) - (mean)
7: sin(2πt)
8: cos(4πt) - mean
9: maybe sin(4πt)
10: log((t+eps)*(1-t+eps)) centered (edge shape but bounded)
But K too large may overfit local. For n=128 coarse, K=6 enough: x, x^2, x^3, x^4, cos(2πt), log edge. Let's use 7.
Center columns by subtract mean to ensure v mean 0. Also scale? Not necessary.
```
def _basis(n):
  t=np.linspace(0,1,n)
  x=t-0.5
  cols=[x, x*x, x*x*x, x**4, np.cos(2πt), np.log(t+0.02), np.log(1-t+0.02)]
  B=np.column_stack(cols)
  for each col: B[:,k] -= mean(B[:,k])
  return B
```
Edge logs can give endpoint weights. If log(t) gives high near0; log(1-t) high near1. This is useful to capture harmonic-like but bounded? Coeff can make 1/t-like if both negative? But softmax with edge log may create endpoint spikes; p0 O? Maybe but local refine can smooth.
K=7.
Need ensure columns finite: t+0.02.

### More robust in full optimizer: re-center v
After updates, softmax invariant to adding constant. We center v to prevent drift/overflow: `v -= np.mean(v)`. If v has large values, clip to [-30,30] after center. This may introduce discontinuity when max > min; but okay. Better clip before exp: `v=np.clip(v, -30, 30)`. Since p~1/n, v range -10 to0; if optimizer pushes some negative below -30, effectively zero. Good.

### Numerical exact line search with `_score_from_v`
This uses `_softmax` which recenters/clips. If v_try large (e.g., 50), benefits. Use m_c passed to avoid recompute next_pow2 each time. Need know n = len(v).
```
def _score_from_v(v, m):
    p=_softmax(v)
    n=p.size
    F=np.fft.fft(p,m)
    b=ifft(F*F).real[:2*n-1]
    if b.size==0 or max<=0: return inf
    return 2*n*max
```
m must be ≥2n-1; if v length same as m, okay. For thread? no.

### Full optimizer with current c and move
Pseudo detailed:
```
def optimize_full(a, max_seconds, seed):
    n=a.size; p0=_proj_nonneg_sum(a)
    rng...
    v=np.log(p0+1e-15); v-=mean; clip[-30,30]
    m_c=next_pow2(2*n-1); m_g=next_pow2(3*n-2)
    p=p0; current_score=_score_c(p); best=p copy; best_score=current_score
    mu=zeros(n); nu=zeros(n); t=0; lr=0.15
    start=time.monotonic(); deadline=start+max_seconds
    last_improve=start; no_improve=0
    progress initial
    # maybe first if current_score > best global update done outside.
    while now<deadline:
       # If p not finite, reset
       F=fft(p,m_c); c=...; M=...; current_score=2*n*M
       if current_score < best_score: update
       if time...
       progress=(now-start)/max_seconds
       temp=0.35 + (0.05-0.35)*progress
       if rng.random()<0.15: temp*=rng.uniform(0.3,3.0)
       w=_weights_from_c(c,M,temp,0.02,0.3)
       g=_grad_for_weights(p,w,m_g)
       pmg=float(np.dot(p,g))
       grad=p*(g-pmg)
       rstd=float(np.sqrt(np.mean(grad*grad)))
       if not finite or rstd<1e-20:
           # perturb
           p = _proj_nonneg_sum(p * np.exp(rng.normal(0,0.2,n)))? v?
           continue
       d=grad/rstd
       t+=1
       mu=0.9*mu+0.1*d; nu=0.999*nu+0.001*d*d
       b1=0.9**t; b2=0.999**t
       mhat=mu/(1-b1); vhat=nu/(1-b2)
       step=mhat/(np.sqrt(vhat)+1e-8)
       step=np.clip(step,-1.0,1.0)
       lr_base=0.15*(1-0.7*progress)
       lr=max(0.02, lr_base) # maybe
       move=-lr*step
       multipliers=(1.0,0.5,0.25)
       if no_improve>5: multipliers=(1.0,0.5,0.25,0.125,0.0625)
       best_trial_v=v; best_trial_score=current_score
       improved=False
       for mult in multipliers:
           vt= v + mult*move
           vt = vt - np.mean(vt); vt=np.clip(vt,-30,30)
           sc_try=_score_from_v(vt,m_c)
           if sc_try < best_trial_score - 1e-12:
               best_trial_score=sc_try; best_trial_v=vt
           if sc_try < current_score - 1e-12:
               improved=True; break
       v=best_trial_v; current_score=best_trial_score
       p=_softmax(v)  # for next loop
       if current_score < best_score - 1e-12:
           best_score=current_score; best=p.copy(); last_improve=time.monotonic(); no_improve=0
       else:
           no_improve +=1
       if time.monotonic()-last_improve>20.0 or no_improve>40:
           sigma=0.05 + 0.25*(1-progress)
           v = v + rng.normal(0, sigma, n)
           v -= mean; clip
           p=softmax(v); current_score=_score_from_v(v,m_c)
           no_improve=0
           last_improve=time.monotonic()
```

Let's write code carefully.

#### Function `_proj_nonneg_sum(a)`
```
def _proj_nonneg_sum(a):
    a=np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    a=np.maximum(a,0.0)
    s=float(np.sum(a))
    n=max(1,int(a.size))
    if a.size==0 or not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)/float(n)
    a[a[~np.isfinite(a)]]=0? 
```
Need handle inf/nan: if any inf, set to 0? Input from our code finite. But robust:
```
a=np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
a=np.maximum(a,0)
```
If size0, uniform length1. Return.

#### `_softmax`
```
def _softmax(v):
    v=np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n=v.size
    if n==0: return np.array([1.0])
    v=np.where(np.isfinite(v), v, 0.0)
    v=v - float(np.mean(v))
    v=np.clip(v, -30.0, 30.0)
    v=v - float(np.max(v))
    e=np.exp(v)
    s=float(np.sum(e))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        return np.full(n, 1.0/float(n))
    return e/s
```
If v all -inf, replaced by 0. If v large, clip. Centering after finite replacement. The order: center then clip. If v has very large spread, clip after center may cap both. Good.

#### `_conv_self_fft`
Use `out`? No closures.
```
def _conv_self_fft(a, m=None):
    a=np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n=a.size
    if n==0: return np.array([], dtype=float)
    if m is None: m=_next_pow2(2*n-1)
    F=np.fft.fft(a, m)
    b=np.fft.ifft(F*F).real[:2*n-1]
    # small negative from roundoff
    np.maximum(b, 0.0, out=b)
    return b
```

#### `_score_unit`
```
def _score_unit(a):
    p=_proj_nonneg_sum(a)
    n=p.size
    c=_conv_self_fft(p)
    M=float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0
    if not np.isfinite(M) or M <= 0: return np.inf
    return float(2.0*n*M)
```
This normalizes; if a unit already, _proj renormalizes, small overhead. For score in loop, to avoid renorm, implement `_score_c_unit(p)`:
```
def _score_and_c(p, m=None):
    p=np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    s=float(p.sum())
    if s<=...: return inf, z
    if abs(s-1)>1e-12: p=p/s
    c=_conv_self_fft(p,m)
    M=...
    return 2*n*M, c, M
```
But in functions we maintain p unit. Use `_score_unit` for initial/candidates, `_score_and_c` in optimizer.
```
def _score_and_c(p, m=None):
    p=np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n=p.size
    if n==0: return inf, empty
    s=float(p.sum())
    if not finite or s<=...: return inf, zeros
    if s !=1: p=p/s
    c=_conv_self_fft(p,m)
    M=float(c.max())
    if not finite or M<=0: return inf,c
    return 2.0*n*M, c, M
```

#### `_weights_from_c`
```
def _weights_from_c(c, M, temp_frac, top_frac, top_alpha):
    L=int(c.size)
    if L<=0:
       return None
    if M <= 0 or not np.isfinite(M):

       # fallback top uniform
```

#### `_grad_for_weights`
```
def _grad_for_weights(p, w, m=None):
    n=int(p.size)
    L=2*n-1
    if m is None: m=_next_pow2(3*n-2)
    pr=p[::-1]
    Fp=np.fft.fft(pr, m)
    Fw=np.fft.fft(w[:L], m)  # ensure
    conv=np.fft.ifft(Fp*Fw).real
    q=conv[n-1:2*n-2].copy()
    np.maximum(q, 0.0, out=q)
    return 2.0*q
```
Check boundaries: conv length m; need q length n. For n=1, L=1, m? n-1=0, 2n-2=0 -> empty. But n≥3. Fine. If n=1, special. We can ensure sequences length≥3. In propose, if n<3, uniform.
Need if conv slice length shorter? m≥3n-2 gives conv length m, slice n-1 to 2n-2 length n. Good.

#### Basis matrix
```
def _basis_matrix(n):
    n=max(3,int(n))
    t=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n)
    x=t-0.5
    cols=[x, x*x, x*x*x, x**4, x**5]
    cols.append(np.cos(2*np.pi*t)-0.5?) # cos of 2πt from 1 to1 mean 1 if full period? Over 0..1, mean=1. So cos(2πt)-1.
    cols.append(np.sin(2*np.pi*t)) # mean0
    cols.append(np.log(t+0.03)-np.mean(...))
    cols.append(np.log(1-t+0.03)-...)
    B=np.column_stack(cols)
    for j in range(B.shape[1]): B[:,j]-=B[:,j].mean()
    return B
```
Actually cos(2πt) at t=0 and1 both 1, mean over sample ~1; subtract mean. Good. Include log left/right. K maybe 8.
But log left+right symmetric U-shape; asym via linear terms.

#### `_coarse_search`

```
def _coarse_search(seed, max_seconds, n_small=128):
    if max_seconds < 10: return []
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    B=_basis_matrix(n_small)
    K=B.shape[1]
    start=time.monotonic(); deadline=start+max_seconds
    # Initial theta list: zero, positive ramp, negative ramp, random
    starts=[]
    zero=np.zeros(K); starts.append(zero)
    x=np.linspace(0,1,n_small)
    th1=np.zeros(K); th1[0]=-1.5; starts.append(th1) # decreasing
    th2=... th1[0]=1.5
    for r in range(16):
       th=rng.normal(0,1,K)*1.2
       th[0]+=rng.choice([-1,1])*rng.uniform(0.5,2)
       starts.append(th)
    starts=starts[:18]
    per=max(2, (deadline-start)/len(starts))
    results=[]
    for th in starts:
       now=time.monotonic(); if now>=deadline-2: break
       alloc=min(per*1.5, deadline-now-1)
       p, sc = _optimize_theta(th, B, alloc, int(rng.integers(0,2**31-1)))
       if finite: results.append((sc,p))
    # dedup/diverse select top 5
    results.sort(key=_score_key)
    selected=[]; sel_arrays=[]
    for sc,p in results:
       ok=True
       for q in sel_arrays:
          d=float(np.sqrt(np.sum((p-q)**2)))
          if d<0.05: ok=False; break
       if ok:
          # LP small if time
          if HAS_SCIPY and time.monotonic() < deadline-30:
             p2, sc2 = _lp_small(p, max_iters=2, seed=int(rng.integers(0,2**31-1)), time_limit=5)
             if sc2<sc:
                sc=sc2; p=p2
          selected.append((sc,p)); sel_arrays.append(p)
       if len(selected)>=5: break
    return [p for sc,p in selected]
```

#### `_lp_small`
```
def _lp_small(p, max_iters=3, seed=0, time_limit=5.0):
    if not HAS_SCIPY: return _proj_nonneg_sum(p), _score_unit(p)
    p=_proj_nonneg_sum(p); p=np.maximum(p,1e-8); p=p/p.sum()
    best=p.copy(); best_score=_score_unit(p)
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    n=p.size
    for it in range(max_iters):
       score,c,M=_score_and_c(p)
       if not finite: break
       A=_build_conv_matrix(p)
       # solve
       res=None
       try:
          res=so.linprog(-np.ones(n), A_ub=A, b_ub=np.full(2*n-1, M), bounds=[(0.0,None)]*n, method='highs', options={'time_limit': time_limit, 'disp': False})
       except Exception:
          try: res=so.linprog(..., options={'disp':False}) except: res=None
       if res is None or res.status!=0: break
       b=np.asarray(res.x).ravel(); b=np.maximum(b,0)
       sb=float(b.sum())
       if sb<=1e-12: break
       g=b/sb
       # try blends
       improved=False
       for alpha in (0.25,0.5,0.75):
          q=(1-alpha)*best + alpha*g
          q=_proj_nonneg_sum(q)
          sc=_score_unit(q)
          if sc < best_score - 1e-9:
             best=q; best_score=sc; improved=True
       if not improved: break
       p=best
    return best, best_score
```
Need `_build_conv_matrix` top-level. For p with n=128, dense list of slices. Use `A = np.empty((L,n))` fill zeros? `np.zeros`. Loop.
```
def _build_conv_matrix(p):
    n=p.size; L=2*n-1; A=np.zeros((L,n), float)
    for k in range(n):
       A[k,0:k+1]=p[k::-1]
    for k in range(n, L):
       i0=k-n+1; i1=n-1
       A[k,i0:i1+1]=p[k-i1:k-i0+1][::-1]
    return A
```
Check for k=n: i0=1, i1=n-1, indices p[n-1? k-i1 = n-(n-1)=1, to k-i0=n-1] length n-1, reverse correct (p[n-1],...,p[1]). For k=2n-2: i0=n-1,i1=n-1, p[n-2? k-i1 = n-1? 2n-2-(n-1)=n-1, to n-1] length1. Good.
Potential large memory if called with n>512. We'll only call n<=256 maybe. Add guard in `_lp_small`: if n>512 return current.

#### `_basic_inits`
```
def _basic_inits(n, rng, max_count=12):
    t=np.linspace(0,1,n); i=np.arange(n)-?; res=[]
    res.append(uniform)
    for c in [0.5,1,2,4]:
       res.append(np.exp(-c*t)); res.append(np.exp(c*t))
    left=(np.arange(n)+1.0); right=(n-np.arange(n))
    res.append(1.0/np.sqrt(left*right))
    for p,q in [(0.5,0.5),(1.0,0.25),(0.25,1.0),(1.5,0.5),(0.5,1.5)]:
       a=(left/n)**p * (np.maximum(1-t,1e-6))**q
       res.append(a)
    # random Fourier
    for _ in range(3):
       a=1.0
       for k in range(1,5):
          amp=rng.uniform(0.05,0.35)/k**0.7
          ph=rng.uniform(0,2π)
          a += amp*np.cos(2π*k*t+ph)
       res.append(a)
    # project, limit max_count after diversity? We can return first max_count projected.
```
Use `np.random.default_rng`. Ensure finite.
Maybe too many; process stage selects.

#### `_diversity_select`
```
def _diversity_select(scored, max_count=5, thresh=0.06):
    if not scored: return []
    scored=sorted(scored, key=_score_key)
    selected=[]; sel_arrays=[]
    for sc,a in scored:
       ok=True
       a=np.asarray(a,float)
       for b in sel_arrays:
          d=float(np.sqrt(np.sum((a-b)**2)))
          if d < thresh:
             ok=False; break
       if ok:
          selected.append((sc,a)); sel_arrays.append(a)
       if len(selected)>=max_count: break
    return selected
```

```
    for sc,a in scored: if not selected and len<max: ...
```

```
def _process_length(n, stage_deadline, best_a, best_score, coarse_starts, rng):
    now=start
    cands=[]
    if best_a is not None: cands.append(_proj_nonneg_sum(best_a) if len==n else interp)

    cands.extend(_basic_inits(n,rng,10))

    for a in cands:
       p=_proj_nonneg_sum(a); sc=_score_unit(p); if finite: scored.append((sc,p))
    selected=_diversity_select(scored,5)
    if not selected and best_a not None: selected=[(best_score,best_a interpolated)]
    # allocate weights
    weights=(0.45,0.28,0.15,0.10,0.02)
    base_rem=stage_deadline-time.monotonic()
    for idx, (sc,a) in enumerate(selected):
       now=time.monotonic(); if now+30>=stage_deadline: break
       w=weights[idx] if idx<len(weights) else 0.05
       alloc=max(15.0, min(1200.0, base_rem*w))
       seed=int(rng.integers(0,2**31-1))
       # cap alloc so not exceed stage_deadline

       if alloc < 10: break

       if sc2 < best_score:
          best_score=sc2; best_a=a2
          print
    # If remaining >60, refine best

    if rem>60 and best_a is not None:
       seed=int(rng.integers(0,2**31-1))
       # interpolate best to n
       a = _proj_nonneg_sum(best_a) if len==n else interp
       a2, sc2=optimize_full(a, max(20, rem-10), seed)
       update
    return best_a, best_score
```

```
def _interp_weights(a,n):
    a=np.asarray(a,float).ravel(); a=np.maximum(a,0)
    s=sum; if s<=0: return uniform(n)
    if a.size == n: return a/s
    x_old=np.linspace(0,1,a.size); x_new=np.linspace(0,1,n)
    b=np.interp(x_new,x_old,a/s)
    b=np.maximum(b,0); ss=sum; if ss>0 return b/ss else uniform
```

### Detailed `_optimize_full` code
```
def _optimize_full(a, max_seconds, seed):
    p = _proj_nonneg_sum(a)
    n = int(p.size)
    if n < 3: return p, _score_unit(p)
    max_seconds = max(1.0, float(max_seconds))
    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed))
    m_c = _next_pow2(2*n - 1)
    m_g = _next_pow2(3*n - 2)
    v = np.log(p + 1e-15)
    v -= float(np.mean(v)); v=np.clip(v, -30,30)
    current_score = _score_unit(p)
    best_score = current_score
    best_p = p.copy()
    mu = np.zeros(n); nu = np.zeros(n); t=0
    start=time.monotonic(); deadline=start+max_seconds
    last_improve=start; no_improve=0; last_print=start
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        # ensure p from v? At start p. After loop we set p=softmax(v).
        score, c, M = _score_and_c(p, m_c)
        current_score = score
        if not np.isfinite(current_score):
            # random reset
            v = rng.normal(0,1,n); break
        if current_score + 1e-12 < best_score:
            best_score = current_score; best_p = p.copy(); last_improve = time.monotonic()
            if time.monotonic()-last_print>30: print...
        progress = min(1.0, (time.monotonic()-start)/max(max_seconds,1e-9))
        temp = 0.28 + (0.06 - 0.28)*progress
        if rng.random() < 0.15: temp *= rng.uniform(0.3, 3.0)
        w = _weights_from_c(c, M, temp, 0.02, 0.35)
        g = _grad_for_weights(p, w, m_g)
        pmg = float(np.dot(p,g))
        grad = p * (g - pmg)
        rms = float(np.sqrt(np.mean(grad*grad)))
        if not np.isfinite(rms) or rms < 1e-25:
            v = v + rng.normal(0, 0.1, n); v-=mean; v=clip; p=_softmax(v); no_improve=0; continue
        d = grad / rms
        t += 1
        mu = 0.9*mu + 0.1*d
        nu = 0.999*nu + 0.001*(d*d)
        b1 = 0.9**t; b2 = 0.999**t
        mhat = mu / max(1e-12, 1.0-b1)
        vhat = nu / max(1e-12, 1.0-b2)
        step = mhat / (np.sqrt(vhat) + 1e-8)
        step = np.clip(step, -1.0, 1.0)
        lr = 0.14 * (1.0 - 0.65*progress)
        lr = max(0.02, lr)
        move = -lr * step
        multipliers = (1.0, 0.5, 0.25)
        if no_improve > 6:
            multipliers = (1.0, 0.5, 0.25, 0.125, 0.0625, 0.03125)
        trial_v = v; trial_score = current_score
        improved = False
        for mult in multipliers:
            vt = v + mult * move
            vt = vt - float(np.mean(vt))
            vt = np.clip(vt, -30.0, 30.0)
            sc_try = _score_from_v(vt, m_c)
            if np.isfinite(sc_try) and sc_try + 1e-12 < trial_score:
                trial_score = sc_try; trial_v = vt
            if np.isfinite(sc_try) and sc_try + 1e-10 < current_score:
                improved = True; break
        v = trial_v
        p = _softmax(v)
        current_score = trial_score
        if not np.isfinite(current_score):
            # reset to best?
            v = np.log(best_p + 1e-15) - mean; continue
        if current_score + 1e-12 < best_score:
            best_score = current_score; best_p = p.copy(); last_improve = time.monotonic(); no_improve = 0
        else:
            no_improve += 1
        if time.monotonic() - last_improve > max(15.0, 0.15*max_seconds) or no_improve > 60:
            sigma = 0.08 + 0.25*(1.0-progress)
            v = v + rng.normal(0.0, sigma, n)
            v -= float(np.mean(v)); v=np.clip(v, -30,30)
            p = _softmax(v)
            current_score = _score_unit(p)
            no_improve = 0
            last_improve = time.monotonic()
    # final eval
    p2 = _softmax(v); s2 = _score_unit(p2)
    if s2 < best_score: best_p=p2; best_score=s2
    return best_p, best_score
```
Potential: In line search, `trial_score` may remain current_score, p unchanged. If no improve, next iteration same p, c same, but temp random changes, gradient maybe different. Good.
If `current_score` not finite after p softmax (due overflow?), reset v from best. Could happen if v clip? no.
Print inside if `time - last_print > 60` maybe. Add:
```
if time.monotonic()-last_print > 60:
    print("  full n=%d score=%.6f" % (n,best_score)); last_print=time.monotonic()
```
But function called multiple times; prints okay.

### `_optimize_theta` similar code, but use B and theta. Need m_c for n. In loop compute p from v=B@th. Score and c. We can set `m_c = _next_pow2(2*n-1); m_g=...`.

```
def _basic_inits(n, rng, max_count=10):
   n=int(max(3,n)); t=np.linspace(0,1,n); arr=[]
   arr.append(np.ones(n))
   for c in (0.5,1.0,2.0,4.0):
      arr.append(np.exp(-c*t)); arr.append(np.exp(c*t))
   i=np.arange(n)
   left=i+1.0; right=float(n)-i
   arr.append(1.0/np.sqrt(left*right+1e-12))
   for p,q in ...:
      a=(left/float(n))**p * (np.maximum(1.0-t,1e-8))**q
      arr.append(a)
   for _ in range(3): ...
   out=[]
   for a in arr:
      b=_proj_nonneg_sum(a)
      # finite
      if np.all(np.isfinite(b)): out.append(b)
      if len(out)>=max_count: break
   return out
```

```
def _spectral_starts(n, rng, count=3):
    L=2*n-1; m=_next_pow2(max(64,4*n))
    out=[]
    for r in range(count):
       t=np.linspace(0,1,L)
       if r==0: target=np.ones(L)
       elif r==1: target=1.0-0.25*(2.0*(t-0.5))**2
       else:
          target=1.0
          for k in range(1,4):
             target += rng.uniform(0.05,0.2)/k*np.cos(2π*k*t+rng.uniform(0,2π))
       target=np.maximum(target,0); tm=max; if tm>0 target/=tm
       T=np.fft.fft(target[:min(L,m)], m)
       a=np.ones(n)*0.5; a[0]=1.0
       # maybe random initial
       if r%2: a=np.exp(np.linspace(0,1,n))
       a=_proj_nonneg_sum(a)

       sqrtT=np.fft.ifft(np.sqrt(T)).real[:n]
       sp=np.maximum(sqrtT,0); smax=max(sp); if smax>0 sp/=smax
       else sp=a
       a=0.7*a+0.3*sp; a=np.maximum(a,1e-12)
       Tnorm=max(abs(T)); eps=1e-10*max(1,Tnorm)
       for it in range(18):

          denom=F+eps
          Fnew=0.5*(F + T/denom)
          anew=np.fft.ifft(Fnew).real[:n]
          pos=np.maximum(anew,0)
          ps=pos.sum(); if ps<1e-9: pos=a
          a=0.5*a+0.5*pos; a=np.maximum(a,1e-12)
          amax=a.max(); if amax>1e12: a/=amax
       p=_proj_nonneg_sum(a)
       out.append(_score_unit(p), p)
    select top? Return first count.
```

### Let's now write final code. Ensure all functions top-level. No nested. No lambdas.
</think>

```python
"""
Search for non-negative step-function heights minimizing
    2 * n * max(np.convolve(a, a)) / sum(a)^2.

The method combines several ideas:
  * FFT-based exact self-convolution evaluation.
  * Low-dimensional logarithmic-polynomial parameter optimization to obtain
    diverse, high-quality coarse shapes.
  * A small linear-program tangent step (inspired by the previous SOTA) at
    coarse length, using scipy.optimize.linprog when available.
  * Full-length optimization in log-softmax space with subgradient/soft-max
    gradient, Adam smoothing, and exact backtracking line search.
  * Progressive lengths (mostly 2048, optionally 4096) and diversity selection.
"""

import numpy as np
import time

try:
    from scipy import optimize as scipy_opt
    _HAS_SCIPY = True
except Exception:
    scipy_opt = None
    _HAS_SCIPY = False


def _score_key(item):
    return item[0]


def _next_pow2(x):
    x = int(max(1, x))
    p = 1
    while p < x:
        p <<= 1
    return p


def _proj_nonneg_sum(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(np.sum(a))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        return np.ones(a.size, dtype=np.float64) / float(a.size)
    return a / s


def _softmax(v):
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = int(v.size)
    if n == 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    v = np.where(np.isfinite(v), v, 0.0)
    v = v - float(np.mean(v))
    v = np.clip(v, -30.0, 30.0)
    v = v - float(np.max(v))
    e = np.exp(v)
    s = float(np.sum(e))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        return np.full(n, 1.0 / float(n), dtype=np.float64)
    return e / s


def _conv_self_fft(a, m=None):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = int(a.size)
    if n == 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    if m is None:
        m = _next_pow2(2 * n - 1)
    F = np.fft.fft(a, m)
    b = np.fft.ifft(F * F).real[:2 * n - 1]
    np.maximum(b, 0.0, out=b)
    return b


def _score_unit(a):
    p = _proj_nonneg_sum(a)
    n = int(p.size)
    if n == 0:
        return np.inf
    c = _conv_self_fft(p)
    M = float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0
    if not np.isfinite(M) or M <= 0.0:
        return np.inf
    return float(2.0 * n * M)


def _score_and_c(p, m=None):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = int(p.size)
    if n == 0:
        return np.inf, np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64), 0.0
    s = float(np.sum(p))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        z = np.zeros(2 * n - 1, dtype=np.float64)
        return np.inf, z, 0.0
    if abs(s - 1.0) > 1e-12:
        p = p / s
    c = _conv_self_fft(p, m)
    M = float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0
    if not np.isfinite(M) or M <= 0.0:
        return np.inf, c, M
    return float(2.0 * n * M), c, M


def _score_from_v(v, m):
    p = _softmax(v)
    n = int(p.size)
    if n == 0:
        return np.inf
    c = _conv_self_fft(p, m)
    M = float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0
    if not np.isfinite(M) or M <= 0.0:
        return np.inf
    return float(2.0 * n * M)


def _weights_from_c(c, M, temp_frac, top_frac, top_alpha):
    L = int(c.size)
    if L <= 1:
        return np.ones(max(1, L), dtype=np.float64) / max(1, L)

    if not np.isfinite(M) or M <= 0.0:
        return np.ones(L, dtype=np.float64) / float(L)

    tau = max(1e-300, M * max(1e-3, float(temp_frac)))
    x = (np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64) - M) / tau
    x = np.clip(x, -60.0, 0.0)
    x = x - float(np.max(x))
    e = np.exp(x)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        w = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        w = e / s

    topk = max(1, int(float(top_frac) * float(L)))
    topk = min(L, topk)
    if topk < L:
        idx = np.argpartition(c, -topk)[-topk:]
    else:
        idx = np.arange(L)

    w = w * (1.0 - float(top_alpha))
    w[idx] += float(top_alpha) / float(topk)
    ws = float(np.sum(w))
    if not np.isfinite(ws) or ws <= 0.0:
        w = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
        w[idx] = 1.0 / float(topk)
    else:
        w = w / ws
    return w


def _grad_for_weights(p, w, m=None):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    w = np.asarray(w, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = int(p.size)
    if n == 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    L = 2 * n - 1
    if w.size < L:
        tmp = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
        tmp[:w.size] = w
        w = tmp
    else:
        w = w[:L].copy()

    if m is None:
        m = _next_pow2(3 * n - 2)

    if n == 1:
        return np.array([2.0 * float(p[0])], dtype=np.float64)

    pr = p[::-1]
    Fp = np.fft.fft(pr, m)
    Fw = np.fft.fft(w, m)
    conv = np.fft.ifft(Fp * Fw).real
    q = conv[n - 1:2 * n - 2].copy()
    np.maximum(q, 0.0, out=q)
    return 2.0 * q


def _basis_matrix(n):
    n = int(max(3, n))
    t = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)
    x = t - 0.5
    cols = [
        x,
        x * x,
        x * x * x,
        x ** 4,
        np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * t),
        np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * t),
        np.log(t + 0.02),
        np.log(1.0 - t + 0.02),
    ]
    B = np.column_stack(cols).astype(np.float64)
    for j in range(B.shape[1]):
        B[:, j] = B[:, j] - float(np.mean(B[:, j]))
    B = np.where(np.isfinite(B), B, 0.0)
    return B


def _optimize_theta(theta, B, max_seconds, seed):
    B = np.asarray(B, dtype=np.float64)
    n = int(B.shape[0])
    K = int(B.shape[1])
    if n < 3 or K < 1:
        p = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
        return p, _score_unit(p)

    max_seconds = max(1.0, float(max_seconds))
    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed))
    th = np.asarray(theta, dtype=np.float64).ravel().copy()
    if th.size != K:
        th = rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, K)
    th = np.where(np.isfinite(th), th, 0.0)
    th = np.clip(th, -18.0, 18.0)

    m_c = _next_pow2(2 * n - 1)
    m_g = _next_pow2(3 * n - 2)

    v0 = B @ th
    current_score = _score_from_v(v0, m_c)
    best_score = current_score
    best_th = th.copy()

    mu = np.zeros(K, dtype=np.float64)
    nu = np.zeros(K, dtype=np.float64)
    tstep = 0
    no_improve = 0

    start = time.monotonic()
    deadline = start + max_seconds
    last_improve = start
    last_print = start

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(th)):
            th = np.zeros(K, dtype=np.float64)

        v = B @ th
        v = np.where(np.isfinite(v), v, 0.0)
        p = _softmax(v)
        score, c, M = _score_and_c(p, m_c)
        current_score = score
        if not np.isfinite(current_score):
            th = rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, K)
            continue

        if current_score + 1e-12 < best_score:
            best_score = current_score
            best_th = th.copy()
            last_improve = time.monotonic()
            if time.monotonic() - last_print > 25.0:
                print("      theta n=%d score=%.6f" % (n, best_score))
                last_print = time.monotonic()

        progress = min(1.0, (time.monotonic() - start) / max_seconds)
        temp = 0.30 + (0.08 - 0.30) * progress
        if rng.random() < 0.20:
            temp *= rng.uniform(0.3, 2.5)

        w = _weights_from_c(c, M, temp, 0.03, 0.35)
        g = _grad_for_weights(p, w, m_g)
        pg = float(np.dot(p, g))
        grad_v = p * (g - pg)
        grad_th = B.T @ grad_v

        grms = float(np.sqrt(np.mean(grad_th * grad_th)))
        if not np.isfinite(grms) or grms < 1e-20:
            th = th + rng.normal(0.0, 0.12, K)
            th = np.clip(th, -18.0, 18.0)
            continue

        d = grad_th / grms
        tstep += 1
        b1 = 0.9 ** tstep
        b2 = 0.999 ** tstep
        mu = 0.9 * mu + 0.1 * d
        nu = 0.999 * nu + 0.001 * (d * d)
        mhat = mu / max(1e-12, 1.0 - b1)
        vhat = nu / max(1e-12, 1.0 - b2)
        step = mhat / (np.sqrt(vhat) + 1e-8)
        step = np.clip(step, -1.0, 1.0)

        lr = 0.28 * (1.0 - 0.60 * progress)
        lr = max(0.05, lr)
        move = -lr * step

        multipliers = (1.0, 0.5, 0.25, 0.125)
        if no_improve > 6:
            multipliers = (1.0, 0.5, 0.25, 0.125, 0.0625, 0.03125)

        best_tr = th.copy()
        best_tr_score = current_score
        accepted_improve = False
        for mult in multipliers:
            th_try = th + mult * move
            th_try = np.where(np.isfinite(th_try), th_try, th)
            th_try = np.clip(th_try, -18.0, 18.0)
            sc_try = _score_from_v(B @ th_try, m_c)
            if np.isfinite(sc_try) and sc_try + 1e-12 < best_tr_score:
                best_tr_score = sc_try
                best_tr = th_try.copy()
            if np.isfinite(sc_try) and sc_try + 1e-10 < current_score:
                accepted_improve = True
                break

        th = best_tr
        current_score = best_tr_score
        if not np.isfinite(current_score):
            th = best_th.copy()
            current_score = best_score
            continue

        if current_score + 1e-12 < best_score:
            best_score = current_score
            best_th = th.copy()
            last_improve = time.monotonic()
            no_improve = 0
        else:
            no_improve += 1

        if time.monotonic() - last_improve > max(4.0, 0.12 * max_seconds) or no_improve > 35:
            sigma = 0.10 + 0.25 * (1.0 - progress)
            th = th + rng.normal(0.0, sigma, K)
            th = np.clip(th, -18.0, 18.0)
            no_improve = 0
            last_improve = time.monotonic()

    vbest = B @ best_th
    pbest = _softmax(vbest)
    scbest = _score_unit(pbest)
    if not np.isfinite(scbest):
        pbest = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
        scbest = _score_unit(pbest)
    return pbest, float(scbest)


def _build_conv_matrix(p):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = int(p.size)
    L = 2 * n - 1
    A = np.zeros((L, n), dtype=np.float64)
    for k in range(n):
        A[k, 0:k + 1] = p[k::-1]
    for k in range(n, L):
        i0 = k - n + 1
        i1 = n - 1
        A[k, i0:i1 + 1] = p[k - i1:k - i0 + 1][::-1]
    return A


def _lp_small(p, max_iters=3, seed=0, time_limit=2.0):
    if not _HAS_SCIPY:
        return _proj_nonneg_sum(p), _score_unit(p)

    p = _proj_nonneg_sum(p)
    n = int(p.size)
    if n < 8 or n > 512:
        return p, _score_unit(p)

    p = np.maximum(p, 1e-8)
    p = p / float(p.sum())
    best = p.copy()
    best_score = _score_unit(p)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed))

    for _ in range(max(1, int(max_iters))):
        score, c, M = _score_and_c(p)
        if not np.isfinite(score) or not np.isfinite(M) or M <= 0.0:
            break

        A = _build_conv_matrix(p)
        rhs = np.full(2 * n - 1, float(M), dtype=np.float64)
        cvec = -np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
        bounds = [(0.0, 1000.0)] * n

        res = None
        try:
            res = scipy_opt.linprog(
                cvec,
                A_ub=A,
                b_ub=rhs,
                bounds=bounds,
                method="highs",
                options={"time_limit": float(time_limit), "disp": False}
            )
        except Exception:
            try:
                res = scipy_opt.linprog(
                    cvec,
                    A_ub=A,
                    b_ub=rhs,
                    bounds=bounds,
                    method="highs",
                    options={"disp": False}
                )
            except Exception:
                res = None

        if res is None or getattr(res, "status", -1) != 0:
            break

        b = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        b = np.where(np.isfinite(b), b, 0.0)
        b = np.maximum(b, 0.0)
        sb = float(np.sum(b))
        if sb <= 1e-12:
            break
        g = b / sb

        improved = False
        for alpha in (0.25, 0.5, 0.75, 0.9):
            q = (1.0 - alpha) * best + alpha * g
            q = _proj_nonneg_sum(q)
            sc = _score_unit(q)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc + 1e-12 < best_score:
                best = q
                best_score = sc
                improved = True

        if not improved:
            break
        p = best

    return best, float(best_score)


def _coarse_search(seed, max_seconds, n_small=128):
    if max_seconds < 12.0:
        return []

    start = time.monotonic()
    deadline = start + max(max_seconds, 1.0)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed))
    B = _basis_matrix(n_small)
    K = int(B.shape[1])

    starts = []
    starts.append(np.zeros(K, dtype=np.float64))

    th = np.zeros(K, dtype=np.float64)
    th[0] = -1.2
    th[6] = -0.7
    th[7] = 0.7
    starts.append(th)

    th = np.zeros(K, dtype=np.float64)
    th[0] = 1.2
    th[6] = 0.7
    th[7] = -0.7
    starts.append(th)

    th = np.zeros(K, dtype=np.float64)
    th[1] = -0.8
    th[2] = 0.7
    starts.append(th)

    for _ in range(16):
        th = rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, K) * 1.25
        th[0] += rng.choice([-1.0, 1.0]) * rng.uniform(0.4, 1.8)
        th[6] += rng.choice([-1.0, 1.0]) * rng.uniform(0.0, 1.0)
        th[7] += rng.choice([-1.0, 1.0]) * rng.uniform(0.0, 1.0)
        starts.append(np.clip(th, -8.0, 8.0))

    per_start = max(3.0, (deadline - start) / max(1, len(starts)))
    results = []

    for th in starts:
        now = time.monotonic()
        if now >= deadline - 2.0:
            break
        alloc = min(per_start * 1.25, deadline - now - 1.0)
        if alloc < 3.0:
            continue
        seed_i = int(rng.integers(0, 2147483647))
        p, sc = _optimize_theta(th, B, alloc, seed_i)
        if np.isfinite(sc):
            results.append((sc, p))

    if not results:
        return []

    results.sort(key=_score_key)
    selected = []
    arrays = []

    for sc, p in results:
        if len(selected) >= 5:
            break
        ok = True
        for q in arrays:
            d = float(np.sqrt(np.sum((np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64) - q) ** 2)))
            if d < 0.045:
                ok = False
                break
        if ok:
            if _HAS_SCIPY and time.monotonic() < deadline - 8.0:
                seed_i = int(rng.integers(0, 2147483647))
                p2, sc2 = _lp_small(p, max_iters=2, seed=seed_i, time_limit=1.5)
                if np.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < sc:
                    sc = sc2
                    p = p2
            selected.append((sc, p))
            arrays.append(np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64))

    if len(selected) < 5:
        for sc, p in results:
            if len(selected) >= 5:
                break
            ok = True
            for q in arrays:
                d = float(np.sqrt(np.sum((np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64) - q) ** 2)))
                if d < 0.045:
                    ok = False
                    break
            if ok:
                selected.append((sc, p))
                arrays.append(np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64))

    return [p for _, p in selected]


def _interp_weights(a, n):
    n = int(max(3, n))
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(np.sum(a))
    if a.size == 0 or not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    a = a / s
    if a.size == n:
        return a.copy()
    x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, a.size, dtype=np.float64)
    x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)
    b = np.interp(x_new, x_old, a)
    b = np.where(np.isfinite(b), b, 0.0)
    b = np.maximum(b, 0.0)
    ss = float(np.sum(b))
    if not np.isfinite(ss) or ss <= 1e-300:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    return b / ss


def _basic_inits(n, rng, max_count=10):
    n = int(max(3, n))
    t = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)
    i = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    res = []
    res.append(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64))

    for c in (0.5, 1.0, 2.0, 4.0):
        res.append(np.exp(-c * t))
        res.append(np.exp(c * t))

    left = i + 1.0
    right = float(n) - i
    res.append(1.0 / np.sqrt(left * right + 1e-12))

    for pw, qw in ((0.5, 0.5), (1.0, 0.25), (0.25, 1.0),
                   (1.5, 0.5), (0.5, 1.5), (2.0, 0.25)):
        a = (left / float(n)) ** pw * (np.maximum(1.0 - t, 1e-8)) ** qw
        res.append(a)

    for _ in range(3):
        a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
        for k in range(1, 5):
            amp = rng.uniform(0.05, 0.35) / (float(k) ** 0.7)
            ph = rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi)
            a += amp * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * k * t + ph)
        res.append(a)

    out = []
    for a in res:
        b = _proj_nonneg_sum(a)
        if np.all(np.isfinite(b)):
            out.append(b)
        if len(out) >= int(max_count):
            break
    return out


def _spectral_starts(n, rng, count=3):
    n = int(max(3, n))
    L = 2 * n - 1
    m = _next_pow2(max(64, 4 * n))
    out = []
    count = int(max(0, count))

    for r in range(count):
        tL = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, L, dtype=np.float64)
        if r == 0:
            target = np.ones(L, dtype=np.float64)
        elif r == 1:
            target = 1.0 - 0.25 * np.square(2.0 * (tL - 0.5))
        else:
            target = np.ones(L, dtype=np.float64)
            for k in range(1, 4):
                target += (rng.uniform(0.05, 0.20) / float(k)) * np.cos(
                    2.0 * np.pi * k * tL + rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi)
                )

        target = np.where(np.isfinite(target), target, 0.0)
        target = np.maximum(target, 0.0)
        tm = float(np.max(target))
        if not np.isfinite(tm) or tm <= 0.0:
            target = np.ones(L, dtype=np.float64)
            tm = 1.0
        target = target / tm

        T = np.fft.fft(target[:min(L, m)], m)
        Tnorm = float(np.max(np.abs(T)))
        if not np.isfinite(Tnorm) or Tnorm <= 0.0:
            Tnorm = 1.0

        if r % 2 == 0:
            a = 0.35 * np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) + 0.65 * np.exp(-np.linspace(0.0, 1.2, n))
        else:
            a = 0.35 * np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) + 0.65 * np.exp(np.linspace(0.0, 1.2, n))
        a = _proj_nonneg_sum(a)
        a = np.maximum(a, 1e-12)
        if a[0] < 1e-6:
            a[0] = 1e-3

        sqrtT = np.fft.ifft(np.sqrt(T)).real[:n]
        sp = np.maximum(np.where(np.isfinite(sqrtT), sqrtT, 0.0), 0.0)
        spmax = float(np.max(sp)) if sp.size else 0.0
        if not np.isfinite(spmax) or spmax <= 0.0:
            sp = a.copy()
        else:
            sp = sp / spmax

        a = 0.7 * a + 0.3 * sp
        a = np.maximum(a, 1e-12)
        eps = 1e-10 * max(1.0, Tnorm)

        for _ in range(18):
            F = np.fft.fft(a, m)
            denom = F + eps
            Fnew = 0.5 * (F + T / denom)
            anew = np.fft.ifft(Fnew).real[:n]
            pos = np.maximum(np.where(np.isfinite(anew), anew, 0.0), 0.0)
            ps = float(np.sum(pos))
            if ps <= 1e-12:
                pos = a.copy()
            a = 0.5 * a + 0.5 * pos
            a = np.maximum(a, 1e-12)
            amax = float(np.max(a)) if a.size else 0.0
            if not np.isfinite(amax) or amax <= 0.0:
                a = _proj_nonneg_sum(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64))
            elif amax > 1e12:
                a = a / amax

        p = _proj_nonneg_sum(a)
        if np.all(np.isfinite(p)):
            out.append(p)

    return out


def _diversity_select(scored, max_count=5, thresh=0.06):
    if not scored:
        return []
    arr = sorted(scored, key=_score_key)
    selected = []
    refs = []
    for sc, a in arr:
        if len(selected) >= int(max_count):
            break
        a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
        ok = True
        for b in refs:
            d = float(np.sqrt(np.sum((a - b) ** 2)))
            if d < float(thresh):
                ok = False
                break
        if ok:
            selected.append((sc, a))
            refs.append(a)

    if len(selected) < int(max_count):
        for sc, a in arr:
            if len(selected) >= int(max_count):
                break
            a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
            ok = True
            for b in refs:
                d = float(np.sqrt(np.sum((a - b) ** 2)))
                if d < float(thresh):
                    ok = False
                    break
            if ok:
                selected.append((sc, a))
                refs.append(a)

    return selected


def _optimize_full(a, max_seconds, seed):
    p = _proj_nonneg_sum(a)
    n = int(p.size)
    if n < 3:
        return p, _score_unit(p)
    max_seconds = max(1.0, float(max_seconds))

    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed))
    m_c = _next_pow2(2 * n - 1)
    m_g = _next_pow2(3 * n - 2)

    v = np.log(p + 1e-15)
    v = v - float(np.mean(v))
    v = np.clip(v, -30.0, 30.0)

    current_score = _score_unit(p)
    best_score = current_score
    best_p = p.copy()

    mu = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    nu = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    tstep = 0
    no_improve = 0

    start = time.monotonic()
    deadline = start + max_seconds
    last_improve = start
    last_print = start

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(v)):
            v = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

        p = _softmax(v)
        score, c, M = _score_and_c(p, m_c)
        current_score = score
        if not np.isfinite(current_score):
            v = np.log(best_p + 1e-15)
            v = v - float(np.mean(v))
            v = np.clip(v, -30.0, 30.0)
            p = _softmax(v)
            current_score = best_score
            no_improve += 1
            continue

        if current_score + 1e-12 < best_score:
            best_score = current_score
            best_p = p.copy()
            last_improve = time.monotonic()
            if time.monotonic() - last_print > 45.0:
                print("      full n=%d score=%.6f" % (n, best_score))
                last_print = time.monotonic()

        progress = min(1.0, (time.monotonic() - start) / max(max_seconds, 1e-9))

        temp = 0.28 + (0.06 - 0.28) * progress
        if rng.random() < 0.15:
            temp *= rng.uniform(0.35, 2.8)

        w = _weights_from_c(c, M, temp, 0.02, 0.35)
        g = _grad_for_weights(p, w, m_g)
        pg = float(np.dot(p, g))
        grad_v = p * (g - pg)
        rms = float(np.sqrt(np.mean(grad_v * grad_v)))

        if not np.isfinite(rms) or rms < 1e-22:
            v = v + rng.normal(0.0, 0.10, n)
            v = v - float(np.mean(v))
            v = np.clip(v, -30.0, 30.0)
            p = _softmax(v)
            continue

        d = grad_v / rms
        tstep += 1
        b1 = 0.9 ** tstep
        b2 = 0.999 ** tstep
        mu = 0.9 * mu + 0.1 * d
        nu = 0.999 * nu + 0.001 * (d * d)
        mhat = mu / max(1e-12, 1.0 - b1)
        vhat = nu / max(1e-12, 1.0 - b2)
        step = mhat / (np.sqrt(vhat) + 1e-8)
        step = np.clip(step, -1.0, 1.0)

        lr = 0.14 * (1.0 - 0.65 * progress)
        lr = max(0.02, lr)
        move = -lr * step

        multipliers = (1.0, 0.5, 0.25)
        if no_improve > 8:
            multipliers = (1.0, 0.5, 0.25, 0.125, 0.0625, 0.03125)

        best_tr_v = v.copy()
        best_tr_score = current_score
        improved = False
        for mult in multipliers:
            vt = v + mult * move
            vt = vt - float(np.mean(vt))
            vt = np.clip(vt, -30.0, 30.0)
            sc_try = _score_from_v(vt, m_c)
            if np.isfinite(sc_try) and sc_try + 1e-12 < best_tr_score:
                best_tr_score = sc_try
                best_tr_v = vt.copy()
            if np.isfinite(sc_try) and sc_try + 1e-10 < current_score:
                improved = True
                break

        v = best_tr_v
        p = _softmax(v)
        current_score = best_tr_score

        if not np.isfinite(current_score):
            v = np.log(best_p + 1e-15)
            v = v - float(np.mean(v))
            v = np.clip(v, -30.0, 30.0)
            p = _softmax(v)
            current_score = best_score
            continue

        if current_score + 1e-12 < best_score:
            best_score = current_score
            best_p = p.copy()
            last_improve = time.monotonic()
            no_improve = 0
        else:
            no_improve += 1

        if time.monotonic() - last_improve > max(12.0, 0.10 * max_seconds) or no_improve > 70:
            sigma = 0.07 + 0.25 * (1.0 - progress)
            v = v + rng.normal(0.0, sigma, n)
            v = v - float(np.mean(v))
            v = np.clip(v, -30.0, 30.0)
            p = _softmax(v)
            current_score = _score_unit(p)
            no_improve = 0
            last_improve = time.monotonic()
            if not np.isfinite(current_score):
                v = np.log(best_p + 1e-15)
                v = v - float(np.mean(v))
                v = np.clip(v, -30.0, 30.0)
                p = _softmax(v)

    p2 = _softmax(v)
    s2 = _score_unit(p2)
    if np.isfinite(s2) and s2 < best_score:
        best_score = s2
        best_p = p2
    if not np.isfinite(best_score):
        best_p = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
        best_score = _score_unit(best_p)
    return best_p, float(best_score)


def _process_length(n, stage_deadline, best_a, best_score, coarse_starts, rng):
    n = int(max(3, n))
    now = time.monotonic()
    if now + 45.0 >= stage_deadline:
        return best_a, best_score

    cands = []

    if best_a is not None:
        cands.append(_interp_weights(best_a, n))

    for p in coarse_starts:
        cands.append(_interp_weights(p, n))

    cands.extend(_basic_inits(n, rng, max_count=8))

    if n <= 4096:
        cands.extend(_spectral_starts(n, rng, count=3))

    scored = []
    seen_arrays = []
    for a in cands:
        p = _proj_nonneg_sum(a)
        sc = _score_unit(p)
        if not np.isfinite(sc):
            continue
        ok = True
        parr = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
        for q in seen_arrays:
            d = float(np.sqrt(np.sum((parr - q) ** 2)))
            if d < 0.08:
                ok = False
                break
        if ok:
            scored.append((sc, parr))
            seen_arrays.append(parr)
        if len(scored) >= 18:
            break

    if not scored and best_a is not None:
        pa = _interp_weights(best_a, n)
        scored.append((best_score, pa))
    elif not scored:
        pa = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
        scored.append((2.0, pa))

    selected = _diversity_select(scored, max_count=5, thresh=0.05)
    if not selected:
        pa = _interp_weights(best_a if best_a is not None else np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64), n)
        selected = [(best_score if np.isfinite(best_score) else 2.0, pa)]

    weights = (0.46, 0.26, 0.16, 0.10, 0.02)

    for idx, item in enumerate(selected):
        now = time.monotonic()
        if now + 20.0 >= stage_deadline:
            break

        remaining = len(selected) - idx
        rem_w = 0.0
        for j in range(idx, min(idx + remaining, len(weights))):
            rem_w += weights[j]
        if rem_w <= 0.0:
            rem_w = 1.0

        alloc = (stage_deadline - now - 5.0) * (weights[idx] / rem_w)
        alloc = max(12.0, min(1800.0, alloc))
        alloc = min(alloc, max(5.0, stage_deadline - now - 5.0))
        if alloc < 10.0:
            break

        sc, a = item
        # After a good first run, subsequent candidates are more useful if
        # they start from the currently best sequence.
        if np.isfinite(best_score) and best_score < sc - 1e-8 and best_a is not None:
            a = _interp_weights(best_a, n)
        else:
            a = _proj_nonneg_sum(np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64))

        seed_i = int(rng.integers(0, 2147483647))
        p2, sc2 = _optimize_full(a, alloc, seed_i)

        if np.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < best_score:
            best_score = sc2
            best_a = p2
            print("    n=%d improved to %.6f" % (n, best_score))

    now = time.monotonic()
    rem = max(0.0, stage_deadline - now - 5.0)
    if rem > 50.0 and best_a is not None:
        pa = _interp_weights(best_a, n)
        seed_i = int(rng.integers(0, 2147483647))
        p2, sc2 = _optimize_full(pa, rem, seed_i)
        if np.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < best_score:
            best_score = sc2
            best_a = p2
            print("    n=%d final refinement improved to %.6f" % (n, best_score))

    return _proj_nonneg_sum(best_a) if best_a is not None else None, float(best_score)


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    """Return the best non-negative sequence found within the time budget."""
    try:
        seed = int(seed)
        try:
            budget_s = float(budget_s)
        except Exception:
            budget_s = 2400.0
        budget_s = max(120.0, budget_s)

        start = time.monotonic()
        deadline = start + max(120.0, budget_s - 25.0)
        rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

        best_a = None
        best_score = np.inf

        try:
            hv = globals().get("height_sequence_1", None)
            if hv is not None:
                arr = np.asarray(hv, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
                arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
                arr = np.maximum(arr, 0.0)
                if arr.size > 0 and float(np.sum(arr)) > 1e-300:
                    best_a = _proj_nonneg_sum(arr)
                    best_score = _score_unit(best_a)
                    print("Previous sequence: n=%d FFT score=%.6f" % (len(best_a), best_score))
        except Exception:
            best_a = None
            best_score = np.inf

        if best_a is None or not np.isfinite(best_score):
            best_a = np.ones(2048, dtype=np.float64) / 2048.0
            best_score = _score_unit(best_a)
            print("Starting from uniform 2048: score=%.6f" % best_score)

        # Coarse low-dimensional search and LP tangent steps.
        if time.monotonic() < deadline - 300.0:
            coarse_budget = min(180.0, max(20.0, (deadline - time.monotonic()) * 0.08))
            try:
                coarse_starts = _coarse_search(int(rng.integers(0, 2147483647)), coarse_budget, n_small=128)
                print("Coarse search produced %d candidates." % len(coarse_starts))
            except Exception as e:
                print("Coarse search failed: %s" % e)
                coarse_starts = []
        else:
            coarse_starts = []

        # Main stage: 2048 points.
        stage_deadline_2048 = min(deadline - 120.0, start + 0.58 * max(120.0, budget_s - 25.0))
        if stage_deadline_2048 > time.monotonic() + 90.0:
            best_a, best_score = _process_length(
                2048,
                stage_deadline_2048,
                best_a,
                best_score,
                coarse_starts,
                rng
            )
            print("After 2048 stage best score=%.6f" % best_score)

        # Secondary stage: 4096 points if enough time remains.
        if time.monotonic() + 300.0 < deadline:
            stage_deadline_4096 = min(deadline - 45.0, time.monotonic() + min(900.0, deadline - time.monotonic() - 60.0))
            if stage_deadline_4096 > time.monotonic() + 120.0:
                best_a, best_score = _process_length(
                    4096,
                    stage_deadline_4096,
                    best_a,
                    best_score,
                    coarse_starts,
                    rng
                )
                print("After 4096 stage best score=%.6f" % best_score)

        # Use remaining time to refine the best sequence at its own length.
        rem_final = max(0.0, deadline - time.monotonic() - 10.0)
        if rem_final > 60.0 and best_a is not None:
            nbest = int(best_a.size)
            seed_i = int(rng.integers(0, 2147483647))
            p2, sc2 = _optimize_full(best_a, rem_final, seed_i)
            if np.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < best_score:
                best_score = sc2
                best_a = p2
                print("Final refinement best score=%.6f" % best_score)

        best_a = _proj_nonneg_sum(best_a)

        # Final validation with the official evaluator when possible.
        try:
            if len(best_a) <= 8192:
                exact = evaluate_sequence([float(x) for x in best_a])
                print("Final FFT score=%.6f exact score=%s n=%d" % (best_score, exact, len(best_a)))
                if not np.isfinite(exact):
                    # Extremely unlikely fallback.
                    best_a = np.ones(2048, dtype=np.float64) / 2048.0
        except Exception as e:
            print("Exact validation skipped: %s" % e)

        if not np.all(np.isfinite(best_a)):
            best_a = np.ones(2048, dtype=np.float64) / 2048.0
        if float(np.sum(np.maximum(best_a, 0.0))) <= 0.0:
            best_a = np.ones(2048, dtype=np.float64) / 2048.0

        return [float(x) for x in best_a]

    except Exception:
        # Guaranteed fallback: non-negative, finite, valid length.
        return [1.0 / 2048.0] * 2048
```
